# V24.01 Client Contract Audit

Generated from the V24.01 client source tree and the current Host service sources by `tools/spec-extract/client_contract.py`.
The client-call rows retain each distinct argument signature, occurrence count, and source location; identical calls are aggregated only when service, method, and argument text match.
Host route presence is compared against service attributes, aliases, explicit route tables, manually dispatched `IService` method sets, and service-name switch routes. Test references are source matches and are not a claim that those tests passed.

## Extraction totals

- Client Python files scanned: 12507
- Invoked RPC and bound-object callsites: 879
- Method references without a direct invocation: 41
- Distinct service/method pairs: 610
- Client notification subscription occurrences/names: 1440/657
- Host literal notification sends: 250 occurrences / 150 names
- Client pairs without a parsed Host route: 72

## Root causes for missing routes and registered behavior gaps

| ID | Calls | Root cause |
|---|---|---|
| D1 | `abyssal_content_manager.GenerateAbyssEncounterContent`, `abyssal_content_manager.GenerateContentFromExistingPlan`, `abyssal_content_manager.GetContentDetailsForShipLocation`, `tourneyMgr.AbortCountdown`, `tourneyMgr.AddPlayer`, `tourneyMgr.BanShipType`, `tourneyMgr.CleanupGrid`, `tourneyMgr.CompletePregameChecks`, `tourneyMgr.CreateMatch`, `tourneyMgr.FinalizeBans`, `tourneyMgr.GetActiveMatchForCamera`, `tourneyMgr.GetFancyDetails`, `tourneyMgr.GetMatchSystem`, `tourneyMgr.GetTeamMembers`, `tourneyMgr.IsOtherMatchInProgress`, `tourneyMgr.LockMemberList`, `tourneyMgr.MatchCompleted`, `tourneyMgr.OverrideError`, `tourneyMgr.OverrideFleetBossID`, `tourneyMgr.OverrideLockRestrict`, `tourneyMgr.OverrideMoveRestrict`, `tourneyMgr.OverrideWarpRestrict`, `tourneyMgr.RemovePlayer`, `tourneyMgr.RemoveSelfFromReferees`, `tourneyMgr.ResetMatch`, `tourneyMgr.ReturnAllPlayers`, `tourneyMgr.ReturnTeamPlayers`, `tourneyMgr.StartAutobanning`, `tourneyMgr.StartCountdown`, `tourneyMgr.TeleportPlayers`, `tourneyMgr.TeleportRefHome`, `tourneyMgr.TeleportToArena`, `tourneyMgr.TeleportToStation`, `tourneyMgr.UnBanShipType`, `tourneyMgr.UnlockMemberList`, `tourneyMgr.UpdateFleetDetails` | These calls come only from eve/devtools authoring or tournament-referee tools. Host has no corresponding privileged content-authoring or tournament-referee service/state; they are outside the player login, station, and space flows audited here. |
| G1 | `charMgr.Add`, `charMgr.InvalidateStationItemsCache`, `charMgr.MultiAdd` | CharMgrService exposes character metadata, asset reads, notes, contacts, and character settings; it has no Add, MultiAdd, or station-item-cache invalidation handler. These calls require a server-side inventory mutation/cache-invalidation path with item change notifications. That path is not wired to this bound charMgr service. |
| G2 | `crimewatch.ApplyTagsForSecStatus` | CrimewatchService covers bound state, safety, duels, and security-status reads. It has no tag exchange operation. Applying tags needs one transaction spanning tag inventory consumption and the authoritative security-status relation; no such callback is wired to this service. |
| G3 | `planetMgr.GetPin` | PlanetMgrService stores and projects a known planet's pin network, but exposes no single-pin lookup by pin item ID. The current service has no reverse pin-ID-to-planet query used by GetPin. |
| G4 | `posMgr.AnchorOrbital`, `posMgr.ChangeStructureProvisionType`, `posMgr.GetConsumptionFactor`, `posMgr.GetMoonForTower`, `posMgr.GetMoonProcessInfoForTower`, `posMgr.GetSiloCapacityByItemID`, `posMgr.GetStarbasePermissions`, `posMgr.GetTowerNotificationSettings`, `posMgr.GetTowerSentrySettings`, `posMgr.LinkResourceForTower`, `posMgr.OnlineOrbital`, `posMgr.RunMoonProcessCycleforTower`, `posMgr.SetStarbasePermissions`, `posMgr.SetTowerNotifications`, `posMgr.SetTowerPassword`, `posMgr.SetTowerSentrySettings`, `posMgr.UnanchorOrbital` | PosMgrService exposes only GetControlTowers and UnanchorStructure. The tower settings, moon-process, silo, and orbital operations in these calls have no authoritative POS runtime/state provider wired to the service, so a reply or mutation cannot be derived from current Host state. |
| G5 | `ship.GetShipConfiguration`, `ship.LaunchFromContainer`, `ship.ResetDirtTimestamp` | ShipService has no handlers for these calls. It has no persisted ship-configuration or dirt-timestamp record and no provider implementing the requested container launch transaction. Returning default values would lose ship settings or report a mutation that did not occur. |
| G6 | `shipCasterLandingPadMgr.GetAllLandingPads` (registered behavior gap), `shipCasterLandingPadMgr.GetFactionLandingPads` (registered behavior gap), `shipCasterLauncherMgr.GetFactionsWithShipcaster` (registered behavior gap), `starterShipcasterTravelMgr.CanCharacterJump` (registered behavior gap) | Host registers all four Shipcaster services, but production construction supplies no landing-pad, faction, or starter-eligibility state provider. The service now reports a CustomNotify error when one of those reads has no provider instead of presenting an empty catalogue or a false eligibility answer. EveJS also deliberately rejects travel while no destination is available; an authoritative Shipcaster catalogue and starter eligibility policy remain absent from Host wiring. |
| G7 | `structureCynoBeaconMgr.CmdBridgeToStructureBeacon` (registered behavior gap), `structureCynoBeaconMgr.CmdGroupJumpToStructureBeacon` (registered behavior gap), `structureJumpBridgeMgr.CmdJumpThroughStructureStargate` (registered behavior gap) | Host wires single-character CmdJumpToStructureBeacon through StructureBeaconTravel and the SpaceSessions jump callback, and wires jump-bridge destination/link reads through the persisted structure directory. It explicitly refuses group jumps and portal bridging to cynosural beacons, and production registers structureJumpBridgeMgr without a CmdJumpThroughStructureStargate travel provider. EveJS implements those group, portal-bridge, and Ansiblex travel transitions; these routes need fleet notifications, portal activation/fuel, access/range checks, and authoritative session settlement. |
| G8 | `structure.JettisonStructureFuel` | StructureRpcServices does not expose JettisonStructureFuel, and the structure inventory/fuel transaction is not wired to this route. A success reply without an inventory mutation and item notifications would be false success. |
| I1 | `DB2.CallProc`, `authentication.Logout`, `cache.Rowset`, `externalQueueMgr.PublishProxyEvent`, `i2.GetItem`, `i2.IsPrimed`, `machoNet.CheckACL`, `machoNet.GetNodeFromAddress`, `machoNet.ReloadClientCodeHash`, `userSvc.ProcessEVESessionCreated` | Carbon or cluster infrastructure call. Host implements the player-facing login/session and MachoNet surface it needs, but does not expose arbitrary DB2 procedures, cache rowsets, external queue publication, node ACL/address/code-reload operations, or the legacy i2 resolver as game RPCs. The callsites are in carbon/common or devtools source, outside eve/client gameplay code. |

## Response-shape review

The route table is a source-level registration comparison, not a packet-level result comparison. Focused source tests cover the populationCap character-entry map, the warsInfoMgr rowset/object-caching forms, lookupSvc location key-value rows, Shipcaster landing-pad ObjectEx/list rows, and jump-bridge lookup shapes. This audit records each route's test source in the Calls table; only tests listed in the validation section below were executed here. Remaining routes without a shape-specific test reference still need per-handler review or an executable client trace.

## Validation recorded by this audit

- `python3 -m py_compile tools/spec-extract/client_contract.py tools/spec-extract/test_client_contract.py && python3 tools/spec-extract/test_client_contract.py`: 4 tests passed.
- Both touched .NET test projects built with 0 warnings and 0 errors; `ShipcasterRpcTests` passed 16 tests with 0 skipped.
- The Host dispatch regression for a Shipcaster call without state passed: 1 test, 0 skipped.
- Under the `eve_p4` DB lock on port 26103, `run-eve-live.sh` ran `test_the_population_cap_answers_through_its_moniker`: 1 passed, 0 skipped. It compared the bound populationCap slot map and rejected move cases with the recorded EveJS scenario.
- Focused `ShipDropContractTests` and `ShipCoverageTests` passed: 3 tests, 0 skipped; the touched service and test projects built with 0 warnings and 0 errors.
- The graphical client was not run.
- The `Test source` column lists matches in test files; it does not report execution state.
